card-capture-layers

Quantity: LumenCardCaptureLayers — One exact live CardPage capture with a shared Target1.a validity mask. (albedo linear / normal / emissive radiance / device Z; card capture atlas (exact pass rect))

Channels: albedo: square(Target0.rgb), sRGB OETF for display; normal: decode Target1.a-gated Target1.xy, reconstruct z; emissive: Target2 HDR (valid zero here); depth: PF_R32_FLOAT raw device Z

Invalid: outside exact pass rect OR Target1.a<=0.5 — navy is not this pass's live card data; black is a valid zero
source: ClearCardCapture clears the card atlases; Target1.a is the source-proved valid flag (LumenCardBasePass.ush:146). A=0 is no card data, not black albedo.

gbuffer-a-perobject-2bit

Quantity: PerObjectGBufferData — bit0 = CastContactShadow, bit1 = HasDynamicIndirectShadowCasterRepresentation. (2-bit mask; per-pixel)

Channels: A bit0 = contact shadow caster; A bit1 = dynamic indirect shadow caster

Invalid: packed word == 0 — cleared/background pixel
source: GBufferClear clears GBufferA to 0; EncodeGBuffer writes PerObjectGBufferData only for non-unlit pixels (DeferredShadingCommon.ush:1003-1004).

Display: {"content_rect_xywh": [0, 0, 1280, 720], "resample": "none", "source_crop_xywh": [0, 0, 1280, 720], "transform": "categorical palette"}

gbuffer-b-selective-mask

Quantity: SelectiveOutputMask — EncodeShadingModelIdAndSelectiveOutputMask: high nibble = SelectiveOutputMask; individual engine mask bits are not re-derived here. (4-bit mask; material)

Channels: PF_B8G8R8A8 byte 3 = packed shading model (low nibble) + selective output mask (high nibble)

Invalid: packed byte == 0 — background/cleared; not a valid shading-model value
source: GBufferB is cleared to 0 by GBufferClear; EncodeShadingModelIdAndSelectiveOutputMask produces 0 only for a zero shading model/mask, which is the cleared background here.

gbuffer-b-specular

Quantity: Specular — GBufferB shader channel Specular (shader .g, memory byte 1). (linear 0..1 UNORM; material)

Channels: R/G/B/A decode for PF_B8G8R8A8: byte 0 = B (Roughness), byte 1 = G (Specular), byte 2 = R (Metallic), byte 3 = A (packed shading model/mask)

screen-probe-trace-hit

Quantity: ScreenProbeTraceHit — Packed hit distance and per-ray flags for screen-probe tracing. (Unreal units (cm) + flag bits; octahedral trace atlas)

Channels: bits 2..30 = float32 hit distance bits (mask 0x7FFFFFFC); bit0 = bHit; bit1 = bMoving; bit31 = bReachedRadianceCache

surface-cache-material-layers

Quantity: SurfaceCacheMaterialLayers — Material layers of the Lumen surface cache on proven mapped pages only. (albedo 0..1 linear; normal unit vector; emissive radiance; depth 0..1; surface cache (physical atlas texels))

Channels: albedo panel: RGB = decoded linear albedo, then sRGB OETF for display; normal panel: card-space normal reconstructed from BC5 RG; emissive panel: HDR tone map; depth panel: PF_G16 / 65535

Invalid: unmapped Lumen.CardPageData page (SizeInTexels.x==0) is masked navy; depth value 1.0 is invalid blue — navy is no mapped page, not a valid zero
source: Unallocated surface-cache pages are cleared to 0; Lumen.SceneAlbedo all-zero is the empty-page marker. Validity is taken from the albedo page, not guessed from normal/emissive alpha.
